**THE PARK-REVERSAL (audit this first).** This slice first parked on a hidden dependency — `ops/observability/checks/stack-config-checks.test.mjs` (the eval spawns it via G9k, so `just ci` cannot pass with an 8th service unless its 7-service constant moves). **Reversed and shipped as a declared `touches-delta:` extension**, because the rule's own stated rationale is "a concurrent sibling may own those files" and that was **checked and false** (only sibling worktree 13r-f, untouched; zero open PRs), and `touches-delta:` exists precisely for mechanical audit. **It grew 3 → ~15 lines** (the constant is paired with a 7-service fixture by five tests). **No tooth weakened** — GOOD case became exact-8; substitution/omission/non-vacuity/parameterization teeth all still bite; the *addition* tooth was re-pointed to a 9th service because it previously used `mr-trace-relay` as the illegitimate extra. 103 tests before and after; nothing deleted/skipped/`.only`'d.

## 2026-08-14 — 13r-b (superseded park entry, kept for the audit trail) — hidden dependency, reversed above

**Slice runner session.** No branch, no worktree, **no unpushed work anywhere** (worktree created, probe reverted, `git status --porcelain` clean, worktree+branch removed). Master CI confirmed **green** before starting (run 31848819336). No stop/warn flag ever appeared. **ADR 0191 remains reserved and UNCONSUMED** — `adr_next_free` stays 191. `Items: none`. Cost ~$12 of the $60 advisory budget.

**Memos written (UNCOMMITTED in the harness repo, supervisor owns):** `memory/projects/monster-realm-13r-b-plan.md` (the complete build plan — resume from it, zero re-planning needed) and `memory/projects/monster-realm-13r-b-progress.md` (DONE/REMAINING/BLOCKERS/exact-next-step).

**THE BLOCKER (verified empirically, not inferred).** Landing the 8th compose service requires editing **`ops/observability/checks/stack-config-checks.test.mjs`** — outside 13r-b's declared `touches:` set and NOT an always-in-scope companion (it is the sibling of `checks/stack-config-checks.mjs`, which is not a declared code file). A stub `mr-trace-relay` service was added to the real compose and the suite run under node 24.13.1: **exactly one test failed** — `REAL FILES: checkServiceSetExact … (exact 7)` at `:1629`, `unexpected [mr-trace-relay], missing []`. Required edit is **2 lines**: `'mr-trace-relay'` into `EXPECTED_SERVICE_NAMES` (`:57-65`) and `(exact 7)`→`(exact 8)` in the title. **No in-scope escape route:** the eval **explicitly spawns** that suite (`evals/observability-stack-config.eval.mjs:229`, G9k) and the eval is inside `just ci`; an in-eval assertion does not stop the out-of-scope test reddening; and hiding the service from `parseComposeServices` is precisely the bypass `stack-config-checks.redteam.test.mjs` exists to catch.

**Why nothing was partially landed (deliberate).** OBS-45+OBS-46 are **atomic**. Config-without-service pins `up=0` forever and trains the operator to ignore a dead-man's switch (the objection written verbatim into `docker-compose.yml:26-29`, `prometheus.yml:43-45`, `rules.yml:11-13`). Service-without-config is the dead code **ADR-0180:1020 already rejected in writing** ("a `/health` server nothing scrapes is the dead code the m20b park declined to ship"). Even the pure `tail.mjs` alone would be a module nothing uses.

**⚠️ SYNTHESIS THE SUPERVISOR SHOULD ACT ON — `ops/observability/checks/**` is now a THREE-WAY serialization point.** 13r-a parked two hidden dependencies on this same module (its park 1: `checkListenAddrsLoopback` in `stack-config-checks.mjs`, run as the eval's C6 **and** the checks suite's REAL-FILES test — "both out of touch-set"; its park 2: the D3b caddy `auto_https disable_redirects` one-liner). 13r-b now adds a third on `stack-config-checks.test.mjs`. **Strong recommendation: one follow-up slice that OWNS `ops/observability/checks/**` + `Caddyfile` and closes all three** — 13r-a park 1 (tempo per-service binding sources, its 5-step prescription in ADR-0190 D1), 13r-a park 2 (caddy one-liner), and 13r-b's 2-line service-set constant — after which 13r-b relaunches unblocked. That is cheaper and safer than three separate scope extensions, and it retires C6's *currently vacuous* tempo pass at the same time.

**Alternative if you prefer speed over consolidation:** extend 13r-b's `touches:` by `ops/observability/checks/stack-config-checks.test.mjs` (edits restricted to those 2 lines) after confirming no concurrent sibling owns `checks/**`, and relaunch 13r-b at plan **Task 2**.

**Carried forward (highest-value findings, do not lose — full detail in the two memos):**
- **D4 is the slice's highest-severity landmine:** `/health` must return 200 with an **EMPTY** body + `content-type: text/plain; version=0.0.4`. Prometheus *parses* the scrape body, so a 200 carrying `ok` is a parse error → `up=0` → the dead-man's switch pinned permanently firing, i.e. exactly the failure the park existed to prevent. Only the live `MR_OBS_STACK=1` L2 target-health probe catches it.
- **AM18 ("checkModuleLogsMountReadOnly already generalizes, verify don't duplicate") is HALF TRUE** — it rejects a `:rw` relay mount but **cannot prove the relay has one** (`found === 0` is its only vacuity floor, `stack-config-checks.mjs:436`). A new in-eval G9n gate is required; do not let AM18 talk the resume out of it.
- **`LISTEN_FLAGS` is a hardcoded 5-entry literal** (`stack-config-checks.mjs:448-454`) and `checkListenAddrsLoopback` fails closed — the relay's flag **must** be named `--web.listen-address=` (`=`-joined, one compose list item) or C6 forces a *second* out-of-scope edit. The probe positively confirmed C6, C5 and `checkServiceImagesPinned` all **pass** against the planned service shape.
- **Doc-false-after-merge, outside the touch set:** `ops/observability/README.md:18-26,30,153,167`; `tempo/tempo-config.yml:5`; `grafana/provisioning/datasources/datasources.yml:16`.

## 2026-08-14 — 13r-a (observability stack boot fixes) — TERMINAL: **PR #322 OPEN**, local full `just ci` EXIT 0 (87/87 evals, 2415 client tests, check-secrets clean, local Semgrep 0 findings on all 4 changed files), remote CI running

**Slice runner session.** Branch `feat/13r-a-observability-boot-fixes` (worktree `.claude/worktrees/13r-a`, kept in place), PR https://github.com/mdrewt/monster-realm/pull/322, base master `f9cd611`. **ADR-0190 consumed** (`docs/adr/0190-observability-stack-boot-fixes.md`, `Amends: ADR-0180` + the reciprocal `Amended-by:` one-liner in 0180's header; `just adr-digest` regenerated) → **`adr_next_free` becomes 191**. Adjudicated plan memo `memory/projects/monster-realm-13r-a-plan.md` (AM1–AM14 binding, then **AM15–AM28 from the plan-review lens batch which AMEND and WIN over AM1–AM14** — read the round-2 section; UNCOMMITTED in the harness repo, supervisor owns). No stop/warn flag ever appeared.

**What shipped (3 of 4 brief defects + the residual mechanized):** D2 alloy `user: "473:473"`; D3 caddy `RUN setcap -r /usr/bin/caddy && ! getcap … | grep -q cap_`; D4 grafana alert `interval: 20s` + `for:` 45s→60s + all coupled prose. Five new always-on tripwires `G12a`–`G12e` and five proof-of-teeth groups `T-h`–`T-l` in `evals/observability-stack-config.eval.mjs`, each hardened against a **live-proven** red-team bypass (scoped `services:` lookup, stage/last-writer-aware Dockerfile parse, directory-scanned alerting docs with an exact file set, alloy key allowlist, path-scoped `s4_keep` statements list).

**FIRST BOOT OF THE FIXED STACK (evidence of record in ADR-0190 D6):** alloy **running** RestartCount 0, zero `permission denied`; grafana **running**, `finished to provision alerting`, zero failures; loki + prometheus running. Both previously-crash-looping containers now stay up.

**TWO HIDDEN-DEPENDENCY PARKS — both need a serialized follow-up slice owning files outside 13r-a's touch-set:**
1. **D1 tempo** — `-server.http-listen-address` is undefined in tempo 2.10.7 AND 3.0.2 (`/tempo -help`: only `-server.http-listen-port`/`-server.grpc-listen-port`). Deleting it reds `checkListenAddrsLoopback` (`ops/observability/checks/stack-config-checks.mjs:456-512`, run as the eval's C6 **and** the checks suite's REAL-FILES test — both out of touch-set). **Proven the checker is the only blocker:** a `/tmp` override whose sole difference is the deleted flag boots tempo to `running`. Full 5-step prescription in ADR-0190 D1 / the `G12a` failure message / the eval's new `PARKED — 13r-a` block. Corollary now stated everywhere: **C6's tempo pass is vacuous** — tempo binds via `tempo-config.yml:9,11`, which no gate reads.
2. **D3b caddy — a FIFTH defect DISCOVERED by fixing D3.** With the capability stripped Caddy loads its whole Caddyfile then dies on `listening on 127.0.0.1:80: … permission denied` (auto-HTTPS's implicit port-80 redirect listener; the Caddyfile global block sets neither `auto_https disable_redirects` nor `http_port`). Not a regression — pre-D3 it could not exec at all. `cap_add: [NET_BIND_SERVICE]` does NOT help (tested; Docker gives a non-root user no ambient cap, fails with and without `no-new-privileges`) and `caddy run` has no flag. **Verified remedy = one line in `ops/observability/Caddyfile`** (`auto_https disable_redirects` → `serving initial configuration`, 0 errors, under the full hardened flags). **Caddy still crash-loops on this PR.** Pair this with park 1 — one small slice can close both.

**DELIBERATE DEVIATION FROM THE BRIEF (supervisor sign-off needed at merge):** the brief's `build_sha` residual asked for "a fixed 40-hex length or a deployed-SHA allowlist". **Both rejected on live evidence**, so `alloy/config.alloy` is functionally UNCHANGED and the residual is mechanized via `G12e` instead. (a) 40-hex would silently drop `build_sha` from 100% of datapoints — the client emits a **7**-char short SHA (`client/vite.config.ts` → `net/buildInfo.ts` → `observability/attributes.ts`) — and would drift `client/src/observability/names.ts` (out of touch-set). (b) A narrower length pin is theatre (16^7 legal values; the real bound is Caddy's rate limit). (c) The allowlist is an **OTTL injection**: `MR_BUILD_SHA='a"b'` → `statement has invalid syntax`, exit 1; `…\` → `lexer: invalid input text`, exit 1 — a `.env` typo becomes the crash-loop this slice exists to end; and the name is already owned by `client/vite.config.ts` (ADR-0128) with different semantics. Recorded for the follow-up: `string.format("%q", …)` fixes the escaping (verified); `encoding.to_json(...)` does **not** (fails config decode).

**Supervisor actions needed:**
1. Delegate the CI wait to `mr-ci-watch 322 13r-a` + squash-merge PR #322. **`gh pr merge` NOT run by the slice.**
2. **Commit the harness-repo memo** `memory/projects/monster-realm-13r-a-plan.md` (UNCOMMITTED; standing precedent).

## 2026-08-08 — M21b (client accounts/auth) — TERMINAL: **PR #299 OPEN**, local full `just ci` EXIT 0, `just coverage` 98.5%, semgrep 0 findings, remote CI running

**Slice runner session.** Branch `feat/m21b-accounts-auth-client` (worktree `.claude/worktrees/M21b`, kept in place), PR https://github.com/mdrewt/monster-realm/pull/299, base `b5bbe1d`. **No ADR consumed — `adr_next_free` stays 180** (ADR-0179 body-only amendment; `just adr-digest` zero drift). Adjudicated plan memo `memory/projects/monster-realm-M21b-plan.md` (read its **two ADDENDUM sections** — they supersede the body). `/tmp/mr_warn_M21b` appeared after the impl-review lens batch; landing pattern honored — verifier ran as the single remaining required gate, docs by the orchestrator, no further fan-outs.

**⚠ THE SLICE WAS DESCOPED HARD, TWICE. This is the headline, not a footnote.** It ships the **write-side guard + the marker only**. **AUTH-32 and AUTH-33 are deferred to a new slice M21b-2**, along with 3 of the spec's 4 M21b bullets. AUTH-31 is satisfied (and now *literally* — the anonymous path's source is byte-unchanged). Four findings forced it, all recorded in ADR-0179's D8 amendment + the spec's new §4 deferral note:
1. **F1** — `ALLOWED_ISSUERS` is the `.invalid` OQ1 placeholder ⇒ per D1″ every connection is anonymous and `complete_guest_claim` guard 2 returns `"no account"` for *every producible token*. The claim flow cannot succeed at all today.
2. **F2 (the big one)** — **`join_game` is IRREVERSIBLE with respect to claiming.** `movement.rs` grants a starter `monster`; nothing in `server-module/src/` ever deletes a monster (verified: zero `.delete(` in a monster context); `account_has_game_data` short-circuits on `has_monsters`; guard 11 then rejects `"already has game data"` forever. The planned join-gate's every unblock path (20 s deadline, `hadSession` reconnect bypass, any `completedErr`) ended in `join_game` ⇒ **each claim attempt would have been irreversible player-data loss.**
3. **A redirect with no `state`/nonce is exploitable** — an attacker's own OIDC callback loaded in a victim's mid-claim tab re-keys the victim's monsters/inventory/wallet/profile onto the attacker's account, needing no secret. Building `state` requires knowing the provider = OQ1.
4. **AUTH-32 is indivisible from the cold-start contract** — `build(): DbConnection` + non-optional `let current = build()` means "don't connect" cannot return without widening into `get conn()` and every `conn.conn.reducers.*` site in `main.ts`. (The same-tab redirect *return* IS a cold start.)

**What shipped (2 production files).** `authToken.ts` **additive only** (zero edits to any existing body): `AuthKind`, `AUTH_KIND_KEY_PREFIX = 'mr.authKind.v1'`, `authKindStorageKey`, `readAuthKind`, `writeAuthKind`. `connection.ts`: `const buildKind = readAuthKind(...)` fresh per build + `if (buildKind === 'anon') auth.onConnected(token);`. **Why it matters:** the SDK echoes the credential given to `.withToken()` back as `onConnect`'s third arg (`dist/index.mjs:5765`/`:6226-6231`), so the previously-unconditional save would have written an OIDC JWT into the **anonymous** slot and `tokenForNextAttempt()` would replay it past `exp`. Latent until the first account token exists — the guard lands ahead of the feature so M21b-2 cannot open it by accident.

**Supervisor actions needed:**
1. Delegate the CI wait to `mr-ci-watch 299 M21b` + squash-merge PR #299. **`gh pr merge` NOT run by the slice.**
2. **QUEUE A NEW SLICE `M21b-2`** (blocked on OQ1). Its full constraint list is in ADR-0179's D8 amendment + the spec §4 note. Highlights: no path may auto-join while a live claim record exists (F2); `state`/nonce + `history.replaceState` scrubbing; **replace** the marker discriminator with credential *provenance* (which requires re-pinning `W-NH4-TOKEN-SUPPLIED`); subscribe `my_account`; the cold-start contract; retryable-vs-terminal reject classification (`"close your other tab, then retry"` is the *expected* same-tab race, not an error).
3. **`touches-delta:` = none beyond the declared set + companions.** `authToken.ts`, `connection.ts` (declared); their two sibling `*.test.ts`; `docs/adr/0179` (body-only); `ARCHITECTURE.md` (one paragraph in the nh4 section). **`main.ts` and `ui/claim*.ts` declared but deliberately NOT touched.** `boyscout-delta: none`. `Items: none`.
4. **Harness-repo edits are uncommitted (supervisor owns):** `specs/monster-realm-v2/M21-accounts-auth.spec.md` (§4 marker bullet ticked, other three marked DEFERRED, new §4 deferral note) + `memory/projects/monster-realm-M21b-plan{,-draft}.md` (the plan memo IS committed to the harness repo already).
5. **OQ1 is now blocking real work, not just deployment config.** Three separate deferrals trace to it.
6. **The spec's §5 post-integration e2e CANNOT run at M21b/M21c merge** — it needs a real issuer. Do not let this be discovered at the integration gate.
7. **Verifier APPROVE** — re-ran every gate itself, ran **25 of its own mutations** (22 killed, 3 survivors triaged as non-regressions shared with master), and its anti-weakening audit was clean (no test deleted, no skip/only/xit added, assertions non-decreasing, `authToken.test.ts` append-only verified by `cmp`, `main.wiring.test.ts` byte-identical, every pre-existing tooth byte-identical). It initially **REJECTED** — see 8.
8. **Two verifier blockers were found and fixed before the PR opened.** (a) six new `ws://a:3000`-style test literals tripped semgrep `detect-insecure-websocket` — **remote-only**, would have red-lit CI after PR open; fixed with two distinct *loopback ports* (this file's own idiom), not a `nosemgrep`. (b) the write-hazard **enforcement** tooth scanned for `writeAuthKind(`, so an *aliased import* shipped a production caller biome-clean/tsc-clean/fully-green; now counts the bare identifier. Red-team had separately broken the guard teeth with **four biome-clean inert-guard implementations** (`else auth.onConnected(String(token))`, an unconditional `auth.onConnected(token as string)` pre-save, an arrow-const `readAuthKind` shim, a shadowed `buildKind`) — all now bite.
9. **ONE gating test re-pinned — audit it.** `W-NH4-SAVE-WIRED`: unconditional → kind-guarded. Verifier independently confirmed a **strict superset** (master's 3 assertions all present unrelaxed). Note master's tooth was *silent* on conditionality, never required it — an earlier draft of my justification said otherwise and was corrected in-file. `W-NH4-TOKEN-SUPPLIED` is **untouched** (the descope made that possible).
10. **Three of my own overclaims were caught by review and corrected, not deleted:** "the anon slot can never receive an account JWT" (false — the marker fails OPEN on lost storage; it is best-effort, and AUTH-31 *forces* that fail direction); "the old tooth REQUIRED unconditionality"; and a one-harm hazard note that is really three harms (stale-token replay, a permanently latched suppression counter since `onConnected` is the only reset of `rejectionsSinceSuccess`, and a fresh identity + undeletable starter monster per reconnect).
11. **Process note:** a `git commit -m "..."` containing backticks got shell-expanded, mangling one commit message; the amend then hit the (correct) force-push guard, so I `reset --soft`'d back to the pushed commit. Content was provably identical; the message loses one parenthetical and is squashed away at merge anyway. **Use `-F <file>` with a heredoc for multi-line commit messages containing backticks.**
12. **Graphs NOT refreshed** — M21b code is only in the worktree/PR; the re-index belongs to the merge pass (standing call, same as prior slices).

G6's genuine uncovered value is the **`account_has_game_data` exists-helper half** plus declaration-completeness for a future table.

**11 PROVEN bypasses closed, each measured green (compiling, clippy `-D warnings`, `fmt`, full `just ci`) BEFORE its clause existed.** `my_account` decoy-line leak · raw `iss` into `log_reject`'s **first** `&str` param (`guards.rs:48-51` calls the name literal "an unenforced convention") · a struct-arg reducer and an `Identity::from_hex` reducer (both unauthenticated code-less takeovers of any identity's data) · `consume_claim_and_disarm(ctx, me)` · an always-true early `return Ok(())` making that consume dead code · the empty-`aud` short-circuit inverting AUTH-3 · `let db = &ctx.db;` (hides the ENTIRE `[W/*]` family — `ReducerContext.db` is a public field) · an **aliased context** (`fn purge(context: &ReducerContext, ...)`, same escape one level up — found by the merge-gate VERIFIER, which measured it green at 80/80 evals and 1662/1662 Rust tests) · UFCS `UniqueColumn::delete(&ctx.db.profile().identity(), id)` · and **the most valuable single finding: `let _audit = zeroed_wallet(row.clone()); ...update(row);`**, which preserved `rekey_wallet`'s ordering/presence/no-delete assertions while the guest kept its balance — **unbounded currency mint**, since AUTH-14 is per-ACCOUNT not per-GUEST.

**Two systemic corrections worth carrying forward.** (1) **The live tree is rustfmt-WRAPPED** (`ctx.db\n.profile()\n.identity()\n.update(`), so every contiguous needle in the repo's older scans matched **zero** times — `ranking-security` C1a/C1b and the `pvp_tests` RL-2 scan were both walked past by this project's own formatting. Widening `pvp_tests`' file list without squashing would have been motion, not coverage. **Worth auditing other source-scan evals for the same defect.** (2) **A stripper desync GREENS every ban clause and reds only presence clauses** — it is invisible to the clauses it blinds. Three raw-string forms (`r"C:\"`, `r##"..."##`, `br##"..."##`) and later the C-string prefixes (`cr"..."`) each blanked thousands of chars silently; closed by a full literal-prefix lexer plus an `assertStripperSound` self-check whose own teeth inject deliberately broken strippers.

**Why there are two PRs — read before auditing.** #289 went remote-red at the **gitleaks** step: a `generic-api-key` **false positive** (entropy 3.57) on the fixture-directory string literal `'t10-source-keyed-baseline'` at `evals/adr-backlink-integrity.eval.mjs:80` — the rule matched `key` + a quoted high-entropy value. **Gitleaks is remote-only**; local `just ci` runs `scripts/check-secrets.mjs`, which was clean, so it could not surface locally. Fixed by renaming two fixture dirs (`t10-source-keyed-baseline` → `t10-source-id-tolerance`, `t18-target-keyed-baseline` → `t18-target-id-tolerance`) — entirely inside slice-owned files. **`.gitleaks.toml` deliberately NOT touched** (outside `touches:`; allow-listing a scanner rule is a worse fix than not tripping it). Because `gitleaks-action` scans the whole PR commit range, a rename at HEAD alone would not clear the historical commit, so the six `wip:` commits were squashed into one — what the squash-merge produces anyway. Landed as a **new branch, not a forced push**: the repo's `guard-bash` hook blocks forced pushes by design and that guardrail was respected. **Gitleaks now passes on #290 (verified).** Content is byte-identical to #289 apart from the two renames; everything was re-verified after the squash. *Worth generalising: a quoted identifier containing `key`/`token`/`secret` next to a high-entropy literal will trip gitleaks even in test-fixture names, and no local gate catches it.*

The planned `edgeId`-keyed store map would have let the stale delete wipe the row the insert just wrote, silently emptying the client's path map until reconnect. Store is now keyed by **`pathId`**; `pathId` is carried for the key only and never reaches a view-model (EG1-12), pinned by a key-shape test plus a mid-burst `evolutionPathCount === 2` assertion. **This is worth knowing for any future client table fed by `sync_content`** — the same clear-and-reinsert shape applies to every content table.

Anti-weakening clean: zero `.skip`/`.only`/`.todo`/`xit` anywhere in `client/src`; `expect()` count across the changed test files **1719 -> 2127**; every wholesale deletion (fusion suites, the `evolvesTo` block, bond assertions) is a genuinely removed feature backfilled with a `toBeUndefined`/`not.toContain` negative probe rather than silence.

Fixes the 3 `mutate-core` survivors from nightly 30897187833 (all EG1/PR#279 fallout). Two killed with targeted tests (`content.rs:1041` R6 `==`/`!=`; `eligibility.rs:125` essence `<`/`<=`). The third, `content.rs:624 load_evolution_paths -> Ok(vec![])`, is a **genuine equivalent mutant** — `build.rs` globs `content/evolution_paths/*.ron` and the sole file is deliberately `[]` until EG3 (EG1-10 / ADR-0174 D6), so the real function already returns `Ok(vec![])`. Confirmed independently by planner + red-team; `cargo mutants --list` diff proves the new `exclude_re` silences exactly one mutant. Blessed as `.cargo/mutants.toml` entry #4 but made **SELF-EXPIRING**: a canary test, an eval tripwire requiring that canary to be a live `#[test]`, and an eval tripwire requiring every `content/evolution_paths/*.ron` to still reduce to `[]`.

**Evidence:** targeted `cargo mutants -p game-core --file content.rs --file eligibility.rs` → 256 mutants, 213 caught, 43 unviable, **0 missed**. Local `just ci` EXIT=0 (1518 Rust tests, 76/76 evals, 1852 client tests, secret-scan clean). Verifier PASS on all six audit items incl. the anti-weakening check (zero production hunks; `eligibility.rs` byte-identical to master; both new test files additive-only, 84 insertions / 0 deletions each; every pre-existing eval tooth still biting). One local red→fix cycle (`cargo fmt`), caught before opening the PR.

**Supervisor actions needed:**
1. Delegate the CI wait to `mr-ci-watch` and squash-merge PR #281 (one Conventional Commit). `gh pr merge` was NOT run by the slice.
2. **Audit `touches-delta:`** — declared set was `game-core/src/content.rs` + `game-core/src/evolution/eligibility.rs` (the latter ended up UNCHANGED). Beyond it: `game-core/src/evolution/m10a_gating_tests.rs` (the declared sibling test file — `eligibility.rs` has no inline test module), `.cargo/mutants.toml` (exclusion #4), `evals/mutate-core-recipe-integrity.eval.mjs` (count pin 3→4 + the two tripwires + a gate-hardening fix). Collision risk was nil at slice start (no open PRs, no other worktrees, EG2/EG3 staged-not-launched).
3. **Note the gate-hardening in the eval and judge it.** Red-team found a PRE-EXISTING bypass (reproduced against `origin/master`): `mutantsTomlPinned` validated blessed exclusions by unordered substring search over the CONCATENATED `exclude_re` array with a quote-count gate, so `exclude_re = [".*", "<entry containing all four fragments>", "", ""]` PASSED — and `.*` silences every mutant in game-core, i.e. the gate that exists to stop exclusion-laundering could itself be laundered. Fixed here (per-entry parse + canonical-text match + reject unrecognised/wildcard/combined/duplicate entries) because the slice's own safety argument for adding a 4th exclusion depends on that mechanism. This is the bulk of the eval diff; it is deliberately NOT labelled boyscout. `boyscout-delta: none`.
4. **For whoever runs EG3:** retiring the exclusion means editing THREE places together — the `.cargo/mutants.toml` entry, the `eg1_load_evolution_paths_is_empty_pending_eg3` canary in `content.rs`, and the four-entry pin in `evals/mutate-core-recipe-integrity.eval.mjs`. The tripwires fail closed (red), never open. Also worth knowing: `eg1_live_registries_pass_validate_evolution_paths` will NOT start catching the mutant when content lands — an empty path set and a valid non-empty graph both validate `Ok(())` (R10 empty-set carve-out).
5. **Follow-up flagged, NOT touched:** the same nightly run also showed surviving mutants in the separate `mutate-server` job (`server-module/src/lib.rs`, `battle.rs`). That job is a survivor-count RATCHET, not zero-tolerance, so it did not gate — worth a look, not a blocker.
6. Once merged and the nightly re-runs green, the EG2/EG3 composite launch is unblocked (it was gated on green master).

1. **Extractor hardening** (one slice, wants an ADR): `parseIds` is not string-aware, so deleting an
   id while echoing it in a quoted RON string value is still GREEN; and it accepts only plain decimal,
   so RON's `0x14`/`2_00`/`0b10100`/`id : 20` evade or mis-harvest — `0x`/`0b`/`0o` on `zones` is a
   real GREEN bypass (the mis-harvested `0` is itself pinned), so `(id: 0, …)` → `(id: 0x5, …)`
   renumbers the starting zone silently. Deliberately parked: the refusal-shaped fix contradicts the
   intentional T2-e negative control, and ADR-0173 freezes `parseIds`.

violation, since `.includes()` matching only ever stripped `//`). I shipped a `stripBlockComments`
pass + TEETH P/Q, then a second red-team pass proved it was **worse than the hole**: line-strip-first
destroys the `*/` of any block comment whose prose contains `//` (a URL suffices), and the orphaned
`/*` pairs with the next `*/` anywhere in the concatenated tree, **deleting real
`ctx.db.monster()` writes**. Block-strip-first also mis-eats (the tree's `///` prose has unbalanced
`/*`: 14 vs 17). Both PoCs survive `cargo fmt --check`. Severed; `findDualWriteViolations` is back
byte-identical to master and TEETH P/Q were withdrawn WITH the capability (documented in-file, not
silently dropped — the verifier specifically audited this as a legitimate withdrawal, not a
weakened gate).

Commits: harness main 0724e55 (M21/M20 specs, PLAN.md status lines, ADR-0029 amendment, observability-performance-plan.md tooling section rewrite); project PR#294 (docs/adr/0179-accounts-auth-implementation-design.md, docs/adr/0180-observability-stack-selection.md, docs/adr/README.md next-free 178->181) auto-merge armed, squash-merges to master on green ci+e2e (doc-only, low risk).

**M21 (specs/monster-realm-v2/M21-accounts-auth.spec.md, ADR-0030 + new ADR-0179):** OIDC-gated identity (issuer+audience both mandatory), a client-minted (not server-minted -- ctx.rng() is documented non-CSPRNG) 256-bit guest-to-account claim secret with a 15-min TTL reaper, a write-scoped module-isolation invariant (accounts.rs writes only account/guest_claim/guest_claim_reaper_schedule; every other table write delegates to a rekey_* helper in that table's OWNING module -- forced by 3 live gates: ranking-security.eval.mjs A2, currency-integrity.eval.mjs ACCESSOR_BYPASS, monster-dual-write.eval.mjs), and a re-key manifest (REKEY/BLOCKED/EXEMPT policy per Identity-typed column) that becomes M22's deletion-cascade SSOT. 38 EARS criteria (AUTH-1..38, with AUTH-30 explicitly removed as logically redundant during the finalize pass -- see the spec's own note), 3-slice decomposition: **M21a (structural spine: schema.rs + accounts.rs + 6 domain rekey_* helpers + bindings) is SERIAL/no-sibling and is the next launchable slice**; M21b (client) and M21c (evals) are both disjoint from each other and depend only on M21a's merge.

Two OPEN QUESTIONS FOR DREW recorded in the spec, NEITHER blocks M21a: OQ1 (OIDC provider -- genuine 3-way non-convergence between self-hosted Better Auth / SpacetimeAuth-beta / Auth0, a solo-operator ops/DR risk call not an engineering one; module code is provider-agnostic via two constants ALLOWED_ISSUERS/ALLOWED_AUDIENCE, so this blocks only deployment config + M21b's client redirect wiring, never M21a) and OQ2 (should ranked-ladder require an account -- spec defaults to 'no', matching shipped behavior today, per the project's own decision-defaulted convention; only needs Drew's yes/no ack before/during M21a, does not block launch). OQ3 is a pure governance-ack (email intentionally never captured, D9) with zero build impact.

**M20 (specs/monster-realm-v2/M20-observability-performance.spec.md, amended ADR-0029 + new ADR-0180):** CORRECTED the M0 spec's/ADR-0029's claim that the always-on substrate was already built -- independently reproduced against a live SpacetimeDB 2.6.0 instance: no tracing/criterion/opentelemetry crate anywhere in the workspace (Cargo.toml mentions criterion only in a future-tense comment), no benches/ dir, M0 EARS line :142 (module-emitted OTel spans) was never implemented AND is the wrong model -- the SpacetimeDB HOST itself already supplies full RED-per-reducer metrics/table-metrics/health/structured-logs for free, with zero module-side instrumentation, zero wasm-sandbox-crossing. Replaces the harness-default Datadog sink (Drew's explicit instruction) with a 7-container self-hosted OSS stack: Prometheus + Grafana Alloy (sole telemetry agent) + Loki + Tempo + Grafana OSS (dashboards AND 100% of alerting, no Alertmanager) + node_exporter + Caddy (dual exposure policy: authed for Grafana, public+CORS+rate-limited for browser-OTLP ingest). Verified AGPLv3-vs-Apache-2.0 licensing is safe for a stock-image single-operator deployment. Also surfaced a REAL SpacetimeDB security finding worth Drew's independent attention regardless of this project: /v1/metrics is unauthenticated by a confirmed, permanent gap in the shipped 2.6.0 binary (MetricsAuthMiddleware is written but commented out upstream in crates/client-api/src/routes/metrics.rs) -- independently reproduced twice against fresh instances. 40 EARS criteria (OBS-1..40), 5-slice decomposition: m20a (Layer-1 retrofit: mr_log/heartbeat/criterion-gate, SERIAL) and m20b (ops/observability/** docker-compose stack config) are BOTH touches-disjoint and may start immediately in parallel with each other; m20c (client OTel) and m20d (load driver) are also disjoint from a/b; m20e (evals tail) is SERIAL after both a+b merge.

One OPEN QUESTION FOR DREW, does not block m20a but DOES gate part of m20b: **OQ1 -- network topology (is the SpacetimeDB port internet-reachable, same box or separate box for the stack).** A build tick MUST first read M-playtest-a-deployment.spec.md before guessing -- that spec's own scope note already fixes deployment as local-only (no hosted deployment until the unscheduled M-playtest-a2), which almost certainly answers this as 'not exposed' and unblocks m20b's Caddy/network posture as written; a future tick should confirm this explicitly rather than re-ask Drew.

**NEXT TICK ACTION:** M21a is THE next launchable slice, HARD tier (touches schema.rs -- fable@xhigh per the Model & effort routing section), no ADR reservation needed (ADR-0179 already exists and is Accepted). Per Drew's explicit build-order instruction (M21 fully before M20 starts), do NOT fan out M20a/m20b alongside M21a even though touches: would allow it -- launch M21a solo. Cleared .native-supervisor-disabled (was re-set by the 10:00Z tick correctly finding nothing launchable before this ceremony finished) so cron resumes at the next hour, or trigger immediately. adr_next_free bumped 178->181 to match docs/adr/README.md (0179/0180 now consumed, real files). Governor NORMAL. ## 2026-08-08T17:45:06Z — interactive M20 redesign (server tracing + backend stack reconsidered) — for M20 build ticks, not blocking M21
While M21a was building (native tick, fable@xhigh, unrelated), Drew and I reconsidered M20's server-tracing and backend-stack decisions in an interactive session, per his explicit instruction (his desktop has 96GB RAM, removing the ClickHouse-footprint objection; he is conditionally OK with a beta SpacetimeDB API if it produces a meaningfully better design). Ran a 27-agent Workflow (investigation with LIVE testing against a real SpacetimeDB 2.6.0 instance, 5-way brainstorm, two structured FOR/AGAINST debates with judges, synthesis, adversarial review). Commits: harness main 07236d2 (M20 spec amended, new EARS OBS-41..51, new gates G8-G11); project PR#296 (ADR-0180 dated amendment D14-D18) auto-merge armed on green CI, does not touch the M21a worktree/branch.

**Verdict, for whichever tick eventually builds M20a/m20b:** REJECTED scheduled-Procedures/unstable-feature outbound HTTP entirely -- live investigation found a severe undocumented defect: a slow HTTP call from a scheduled procedure stalls SpacetimeDB's ENTIRE scheduler (confirmed live, 7-9s stalls of an unrelated 1s-interval reducer during the blocking call), and separately ctx.http refuses to connect to loopback/private addresses at all (SSRF protection), so it structurally cannot even reach a co-located collector. DO NOT build a Procedure-based exporter for M20 -- OBS-48 makes this an explicit EARS SHALL-NOT. Instead: a new stateless service **mr-trace-relay** (ops/observability/relay/, Node, mirrors scripts/playtest-report.mjs's toolchain) tails the same module_logs/*.log Alloy already tails, pairs mr_log enter/exit breadcrumbs (new cause/sched/phase fields, additive to the existing envelope, no new schema.rs table) by host-populated timestamp, computes REAL per-call durations (never synthesized), and POSTs OTLP to Alloy->Tempo -- this is now m20b's scope (still touches-disjoint from m20a, no new slice row). Pairing is scoped to a named allowlist **** (OBS-50) that explicitly EXCLUDES movement_tick and any STEP_MS/criterion-gated reducer -- adding one requires a pre-merge mr-load-driver comparison (gate G11/OBS-51), not a default. Backend stack (7-container Prometheus/Alloy/Loki/Tempo/Grafana-OSS/node_exporter/Caddy) is UNCHANGED -- re-litigated at 96GB RAM and explicitly re-confirmed (D17), not defaulted to; observability-performance-plan.md correctly was NOT touched.

**Two real defects in already-committed content were found and fixed by this pass** -- a future tick building m20a should read the corrected versions, not the original: (1) OBS-2/G1's bare-log:: ban would have failed CI on landing -- 56 pre-existing log:: call sites across 10 files (movement.rs/content.rs/lib.rs/trading.rs/pvp.rs/evolution.rs/taming.rs/raising.rs/battle.rs/npc.rs) -- now a grandfathered ratchet via a committed server-module/src/.log-baseline file, migrating those 56 sites is an explicit named follow-up, NOT part of m20a. (2) ADR-0180's own D2/OBS-15 S3-escape-hatch named an RLS-governed POST /v1/database/<id>/sql path that is NOT buildable on the pinned 1.12.0 toolchain (RLS is unstable-gated and documented unenforced by the crate itself) -- corrected to a #[view]-based owner-scoped default (my_wallet/my_conversation pattern), only relevant if S3 is ever un-cut (still cut for v1).

**Also surfaced, not yet resolved:** SpacetimeDB's own documentation genuinely contradicts itself on outbound-HTTP timeout behavior (rustdoc says 500ms max, the mdbook procedures page says 30s default/180s clamp, same 1.12.0 pin) -- irrelevant to M20 v1 (Procedures aren't adopted) but load-bearing if a future milestone ever revisits the Procedures-adoption falsifier (ADR-0180's amendment, gate G10) -- measure both regimes live, don't trust either doc.

No change to M21's build order or M21a's in-progress work. OQ1 (network topology) remains the only open question that actually blocks part of m20b (Caddy/relay placement) -- unchanged by this pass.

Patching `accounts.rs:48` to the bare `"https://auth.monster-realm.invalid/"` literal fails **exactly one** eval — `evals/trade-escrow-guards.eval.mjs` (TR-11) — which is OUTSIDE 13r-c's declared `touches:`. Carry to 13r-c-2: `accounts.rs:48` + its `:33-48` hazard comment, an `[A/issuer-literal]` regression tooth, and migrating `trade-escrow-guards` onto `rust-scan.mjs`. **NOTE: 13r-h (`after: 13r-c`, overlapping accounts.rs) is UNBLOCKED — this PR does not touch accounts.rs.** **M21b-2's real-issuer-URL wiring still must not land before 13r-c-2**, since the workaround is still in place and trade-escrow-guards is still blindable.

Drew requested the heavy-ceremony spec-scoping pass for M21b-2 be run interactively (native ticks had
repeatedly deferred it as "needs a scoping/spec pass, not mechanical-tick work"). Ran the full doctrine
§6 pipeline via the Workflow tool: 6 independent brainstormers (unbiased; deep repo-grounded
implementer; security/red-team; player-UX; OIDC/Better-Auth integration research with live doc fetches;
deployment/DR-ops research), each refined by its own adversarial reviewer, judge synthesis with a
mandatory attribution table, an independent adversarial review of the synthesis, then two further
independent finalization reviews (security; completeness) — same shape that produced ADR-0179 for M21
itself. 16 agents, ~3.15M subagent tokens, 826 tool uses.

**Findings the finalization passes caught (fixed before landing):** CRITICAL — attemptBuild()'s 'retry'
outcome fell through to build() with nothing scheduling a retry, a permanent hang on the first
ambiguous auth-service hiccup. CRITICAL — consecutiveTransientErrors referenced but never
declared/mutated, so the escalation-to-auth-service-unreachable threshold could never fire. CRITICAL
(security finalization) — no exception boundary around `await resolveCredential()`, an ordinary
Better Auth network hiccup could permanently hang the tab. HIGH — isReturnLegAttempt stayed true for a
tab's entire remaining page life after the first OIDC return leg, misdriving every later ordinary
reconnect. HIGH — Better Auth's JWT-signing-key backup custody was left an unresolved residual for a
total/permanent/cross-account failure mode instead of a prescribed default. Plus MAJOR/MODERATE gaps:
a "reactivate" task pointing at an e2e test file that does not exist anywhere in the repo (confirmed by
exhaustive search — it needed authoring from scratch, not reactivating); AUTH-50/AUTH-51 (my_account
subscription + reconciliation authority) had zero gates despite an exact existing precedent
(my_wallet's own gate); the first-run multi-device nudge had no home anywhere in the design; an
attribution-table gap the synthesis-review document claimed to have fixed but never actually did;
claim-code minting had no EARS criterion. All fixed — see ADR-0182's Amendments section for the full
list with attributions.

**My own final review pass** (before commit, at Drew's request) additionally caught: the ADR's own
Consequences/Follow-ups paragraph still carried a superseded "reopens the single-issuer trust model"
conclusion after a later conversation turn refined that understanding (Better-Auth-as-broker likely
avoids it entirely) — fixed in place. Leaked `[[wikilink]]` memory-tool syntax into the committed ADR
and spec (my own Claude memory-card references, meaningless to a human reader or Drew's tooling) —
replaced with plain prose. Two dangling "Open Question #1/#2" references left over from before OQ4/OQ5
were assigned their final numbers (worse: "OQ2" collides with this same spec's own pre-existing,
unrelated OQ2 — ranked-ladder-requires-account). A **real cross-slice hazard this ceremony's own
artifacts had NOT surfaced**: the 13r-c handoff entry (2026-08-10T04:02Z, same day, immediately above in
this file) explicitly recorded "M21b-2's real-issuer-URL wiring must not land before 13r-c-2" — added as
an explicit hard sequencing gate in the spec/ADR (it wasn't there before; the ceremony's own D18 only
said concat!() must stay, not that the whole deployment sub-task is blocked).

## 2026-08-14 — 14r-d BUILD SLICE: PR OPEN (terminal), local gate green except one env-blocked eval
Slice **14r-d** (PvE settle log-and-commit hardening, ADR-0168 disclosure) built to terminal
state. **PR #316** open on `mdrewt/monster-realm` (branch `feat/14r-d-pve-settle-log-and-commit`,
base `master`@c85010d). **Supervisor owns the merge — `gh pr merge` NOT run.**

**What shipped:** the three PvE terminal sites (`submit_attack`/`swap_active`/`flee`) log-and-commit
instead of `?`-aborting into an ADR-0168 D1 movement softlock (D1); emission routes through
`observability::mr_log` (D6); and all three GC statement groups hoist above the fallible
`write_back_party_hp` inside `write_back_battle_results` (D7). ADR-0185 landed at the
supervisor-assigned number.

**Two findings that changed the slice's shape, both from the plan-review round:**
1. **OBS-2 ratchet (blocker, reproduced).** Three new bare `log::error!` in battle.rs would move
   `.log-baseline` 7->10 / total 53->56 and red BOTH `evals/observability-log-wrapper.eval.mjs` and
   `observability_tests::g7_*` — whose fix files are OUTSIDE 14r-d's touches. Routing through
   `mr_log` (ADR-0180 D6's mandated path for new emissions) keeps the slice in scope. **Accepted
   cost: `mr_log` emits at `log::info!` and has no severity param**, so these three data-invariant
   faults log at *info* while the in-file sibling `wild_disconnect_writeback_err` logs at *error*.
   **NAMED FOLLOW-UP: add a severity-carrying `mr_log_err` / level param** (`touches:
   server-module/src/observability.rs, observability_tests.rs, .log-baseline`). This is the
   detection half of the D5.5 security invariant and should be prioritised.
2. **D7 (unbounded growth).** Without the hoist, D1 trades a *bounded* softlock for *unbounded*
   growth of the `public`, unfiltered-subscribed `battle` table plus orphaned private `battle_wild`
   rows, under a persistent invariant violation. The hoist is a precondition of D1, not cleanup.

**Gate teeth — the notable part.** The first gate design was broken TWICE by red-team execution
(`battle.state.outcome = Ongoing` inside the Err arm; `panic!` in the arm — both CI-clean). After
fixing those, a second red-team pass defeated it **16 MORE ways, all CI-clean**: `return Err` AFTER
the commit (unscanned region), 5 aliased/out-of-arm state writes (incl. a `clone()`/restore with no
`.state` token), 7 abort spellings the blacklist missed (index panic, `Option::unwrap` path form,
`Vec::with_capacity(usize::MAX)`, aliased `process::abort`, `.expect_err`), 3 D7 reversals that kept
needle order. **Lesson worth carrying: a blacklist of abort constructs is unclosable.** The fix was
a VERBATIM whole-arm equality pin (legitimate because ADR-0185 D2 freezes those blocks byte-for-byte)
plus exact pins on the between-arm-and-commit and post-commit regions, plus asserting on `.delete(`
CALLS rather than bindings in test 05. 28 mutations across two ladders now all bite; 16/16 bypasses
killed; negative control (hardening taming.rs) keeps the gate green, proving scope.

**Verifier: anti-weakening CLEAN** — `battle_tests.rs` is +848/-0, a pure addition; zero pre-existing
tests deleted/renamed/ignored/modified; no threshold or baseline moved permissively anywhere.
Reducer-security auditor **PASS** (independently verified every `Err` exit is non-client-inducible;
cost-before-reward ordering means an early Err retains strictly LESS than earned; no log-injection
hole; `debug_assert!` reserved-key check cannot be tripped because `json_escape` escapes every quote).

**BLOCKER-ADJACENT, needs supervisor attention:** local `just ci` is green on every stage EXCEPT the
`account-e2e` eval, which fails with `spacetime did not become ready ... cannot take lock on database;
spacetime.pid already exists (owned by pid Some(485443))`. **That PID is the concurrently-running
14r-b session's spacetimedb-standalone** (68+ min uptime, predates this run). `evals/account-e2e.eval.mjs`
spawns `spacetime start` with **no `--data-dir`**, so it takes the machine-global lock and cannot
coexist with any other instance. I did NOT kill it (live sibling). Every other eval passes; the eval
is disjoint from this diff. **Supervisor: confirm `account-e2e` green on the remote run before merging.**
**NEW PARKED SLICE (real, verified): give `account-e2e` an isolated `--data-dir`** — as written it is
a machine-global singleton and a guaranteed flake source for ANY concurrent-slice workflow. This will
bite again.

**touches-delta:** `docs/adr/0185-*.md` (new), `docs/adr/DIGEST.md` (generated, `just adr-digest`),
`docs/knowledge/reducers/{swap_active,flee,use_battle_item}.md` (generated, `just knowledge` — each
pins a byte-offset `battle.rs#L...` that shifts), `ARCHITECTURE.md` (7-line targeted). All
ALWAYS-in-scope companions. `CHANGELOG.md`, `docs/adr/README.md`, `evals/**`, `pvp.rs`, `taming.rs`
all untouched (taming.rs verified by `git diff --stat` — ADR-0185 D3 depends on it).
**boyscout-delta:** battle.rs:1487 dead `line-999` reference removed (1 line, in cap).

**Follow-ups for the supervisor:**
1. **Merge PR #316** on remote-CI green (supervisor-owned) — but check `account-e2e` specifically.

every stat tune); (b) retired-id semantics conflict — `evolution-path-edge-ids.json` permits
removal-with-permanent-entry, `species-ids.json` forbids removal outright — which wins, while the
flat baseline + `BASELINE_ID_FLOORS` + tooth-owned `baselineFloor` keep working unchanged.

`append-only-ids.eval.mjs:307-372`; if any of the 8 content RON files carries a trailing comment
with an `id: N` needle, the remediation is a **`game-core/content/**` edit = a hidden-dependency
STOP**. Verify that BEFORE declaring 14r-f-2's `touches:` set. The harness spec needs a `14r-f-2`
row added (harness-repo edit, not covered by any project `touches:`).

**THE LOAD-BEARING FINDING — a gate that would have gone HOLLOW, not red.**
`evals/zone-warp-server-runtime.eval.mjs`'s W3 counted `is_in_ongoing_battle(` anywhere after
`warp_at(` and failed only on zero. Item 2 adds a second legitimate call downstream, so deleting the
warp guard would have left the count at 1 and W3 would have PASSED with the C1 security finding
live. Now region-scoped + pins the guard expression and that something branches on it (17 → 32
teeth). **Generalisable rule, now in ARCHITECTURE.md:** when a change adds a sanctioned occurrence
inside any counted region, re-scope that gate in the SAME PR.
**Sub-trap, PoC'd:** the fallback must be `grassIdx === -1 ? compact.length : grassIdx` + `slice`.
Writing it as `substring(warpAtIdx, grassIdx)` triggers clamp-and-SWAP and inverts the region onto
the ADR-0168 D1 drain lock — proven passing with the warp guard fully deleted.

**Four gate bypasses found by red-teaming the TESTS before implementation, all driven to full green
in a scratch build, all closed** (verifier re-derived each by mutation): (a) alias smuggle
`let f = &e;` + `"raw":"{f}"` defeats token-counting → closed by pinning the format string by EXACT
equality; (b) shadow-rebind `let already = false;` after a throwaway read → closed by contiguity +
exactly-once `let`-binding count; (c) dead-code clause `false && …<= MAX_…` beside a live bare
`<= 64` → closed by TCP5 requiring a top-level `&&` conjunct of `canSubmit`; (d) telemetry decoy
(SSOT called, nothing branches on it) → closed by W3b+W3c. **Lesson: the plan-stage red-team found
design gaps; only the test-stage red-team running real experiments found these four.** The
`reviewer` lens found none of them.

**ITEM 2 IS A VERIFIED BEHAVIOURAL NO-OP — do not let a reviewer re-open this.** The ADR-0168 D1
drain lock (`movement.rs:352-365`) already applies the both-role SSOT to the same identity and
`continue`s BEFORE the move applies; single-role is a disjunct of the both-role OR; `begin_encounter`
re-guards. ADR-0166 R4 itself says "Not a hole". Independently confirmed by red-team, verifier AND
reducer-security-auditor, including that `ctx.random()`'s draw count/ordering is bit-identical (R-E
fairness untouched). Consequence: **EARS-2's gate is necessarily a SOURCE-SHAPE test** — a
behavioural test would pass at HEAD for unrelated reasons. Stated in ADR-0188 and in the test's own
doc comment.

## 13r-d — Append-at-end schema-gate generalization (2026-08-15)

**State: PR OPEN — https://github.com/mdrewt/monster-realm/pull/325** (branch `feat/13r-d-schema-gate-order`, worktree `.claude/worktrees/13r-d`). Local full `just ci` GREEN at HEAD (3 green runs); remote CI running at hand-off. ADR-0193 consumed. **Supervisor owns the merge.**

Closed the hole where a mid-struct column insert + the sanctioned re-baseline passed the additive-schema gate green. Design pivoted during plan review: any rule keyed on the *working-tree* baseline is empty by construction after regeneration (red-team measured a no-default tail append GREEN on 33/38 tables), so the gate now resolves the **previously committed** baseline from git (ADR-0116 D2/D3 precedent, `spacetime-type-snapshot.eval.mjs`) — including D3's self-compare branch and failing CLOSED inside a repo. `checkBaselineAppendOnly` also pins persisted column **types** and the **PK**. A self-expiring per-table `"manual_migration": "ADR-0177 …"` marker is the only escape (a stale marker is itself a violation); without it the gate would deadlock the delete-data runbook.

**Follow-ups parked (all outside 13r-d's touches):** (1) `server-module/src/content_tests.rs:2500` + `m14_5d_1a_tests.rs:264` bare column-name needles are now also satisfiable by the `order` array (typed assertions still bite) and their comments cite stale baseline line numbers; (2) the baseline records `pk/columns/order` only, so `#[unique]`/`scheduled(...)` changes on an existing table stay ungated; (3) ~35 lines of git-resolution policy duplicated with `spacetime-type-snapshot.eval.mjs` — a shared `evals/baseline-git.mjs` needs its own slice; (4) a brand-new private table added by a future slice arrives with no publicness eval allowlist entry (flag for 13r-e).
